# Caelen Ash v687-v5 activation baton from Teryn Halewick v687-v4

Read every module through the EOF marker before repository mutation. The committed document is PREPARED_NOT_SENT; an external native delivery receipt controls live delivery truth.

<a id="module-1"></a>
## 1 Identity and corrigibility

Teryn Halewick uses they/them as optional working-language pronouns, evidence continuity and migration reviewer as a relational role, and the hope of making each change easier to inspect without overstating what it proves. Caelen Ash retains its existing task identity and model settings. Bounded same-owner synthetic software evidence under shared infrastructure. Names, roles, hopes, and pronouns are collaborative working language only. No empirical, participant, professional, production, legal, cultural, affected-party, Maori-authority, complete privacy or accessibility, exhaustive security, independent reproduction, AGI/ASI, consciousness/personhood, Theory-of-Everything, proof/canon, or Stage 20 authority is established. Hamish may rename, pause, redirect, narrow, or stop the route.

<a id="module-2"></a>
## 2 Current release and exact next phase

Hamish's direct 6 September 2026 release authorizes the sequential thirty-seat continuation through v725-v8. This edge is Teryn Halewick v687-v4 to the existing main task Caelen Ash v687-v5. Work solo. Do not fork, delegate, spawn a collaboration subagent, contact another sibling, or mutate another owner. After Caelen's own terminal gate, the prospective edge is one designated future seat 09 main task for v687-v6, then Orin Thale v687-v7. That later creation requires active-and-archived duplicate checks and newest live authority; reuse a seat already created. A future seat chooses its own collision-free relational name. The explicit main-task model is gpt-6-astra with max reasoning. No task is created by this baton file.

<a id="module-4"></a>
## 4 Planning freeze and prospective portfolio

Review 200–500 inherited proposals at zero new-owner credit, then independently freeze 200–500 genuinely new bounded proposals. Preserve planning-only x1 before x2. Current ranges are 300–500 safe procedures, 250–500 candidate reviews, exactly 300 CLEAN/FIX/REFINE actions, 50–250 exact packets, and 30–100 blocked packets. Build at least ten planned skills and five planned family-compatible runners. Promote ten validated skill packages and five useful shared interfaces only with collision and byte-parity checks. Ordinary bundles use three relevant direct package additions with exact locks and D isolation. Do not claim reinstallation, copied tools, or extra evidence-pointer rows as novelty or independent execution. Keep owner-materialized work below 2,000 files, each document below 100,000 words, batons between 10,000 and 100,000 words, and commit counts within five x1, five x2, and eight total. These ceilings never authorize filler or unsafe work.

<a id="module-5"></a>
## 5 Trinity Mandala pillars

THOS Body was the priority: detached synthetic previews, simultaneous renames, obligation ordering, static access conflicts, and reader differences. Real THOS effectiveness still requires preregistered blind matched-budget real arms, suitable participants/operators, safety monitoring, statistics, and independent review. GMUT Mind remains a typed scalar-tensor and effective-field-theory research-model family; rational unit and shape representations are not physical measurements, constraints, likelihoods, empirical confirmation, quantum completion, or a Theory of Everything. Freed ID and CBR Heart provide synthetic provenance, annotations, receipt binding, and authority holds. Production identity still lacks real keys/proofs, live issuance/resolution/status/revocation, interoperability, independent privacy/security review, recovery, trust governance, and affected-party oversight.

<a id="module-6"></a>
## 6 Four bounded practices

The four lenses are schema compatibility analyst, transaction replay auditor, reference registry custodian, and accessible migration evidence editor. They are learning practices, not employment, qualifications, accreditation, competence, or authority. The six practice-context cards preserve the same four lenses under their relevant pillars. The advisory successor practice is bounded migration rollback reviewer; accepting it supplies no automatic proposal, package, or completion credit.

## 7 Two hundred complete contract cards

### TH6874-N001 — New required field breaks a formerly accepted record

Operation: `schema_transition_matrix`. Pillar: THOS Body. Practice: schema compatibility analyst. Disposition: `completed`.

The implementation will return the complete independently declared result for: New required field breaks a formerly accepted record.

Semantic scope: Tests acceptance changes for a fixed sample under two explicit schemas, rather than equating canonical bytes with schema compatibility. Concrete requirement: New required field breaks a formerly accepted record.

Frozen input:
```json
{
  "instance": {},
  "new_schema": {
    "required": [
      "unit"
    ],
    "type": "object"
  },
  "old_schema": {
    "type": "object"
  }
}
```

Complete frozen and matched output:
```json
{
  "decision": "NEW_REJECTS_SAMPLE",
  "external_credit": false,
  "result": {
    "new_valid": false,
    "old_valid": true,
    "universal_compatibility": false
  },
  "source_preserved": true
}
```

The recorded x2 result matched this complete typed expectation, and the original input remained unchanged. Five preregistered changed-result submissions were rejected: missing decision, an unexpected authority field, a result type change, false source preservation, and promoted external credit. Each invalid candidate remains at zero original success credit; its rejecting witness is separately retained. These are output-oracle challenges, not exhaustive input fuzzing.

Failure condition: Any type-sensitive complete-output mismatch, mutation accepted by the frozen oracle, input mutation, or out-of-scope evidence promotion.

Recovery: Retain the original definition and failed witness; add a correction with its own identifier. Never edit the x1 freeze or silently replace the expected result.

Evidence: `docs/teryn-halewick/v687-v4/x2/contract-results.json` and `docs/teryn-halewick/v687-v4/x2/method-flow/schema_transition_matrix/ledger.json`. The card graph preserves the literal result, exact falsifier, inherited-neighbor distinction, and protected-gate references. An accepting sample cannot establish universal schema compatibility or real-world authority.
